## String References

- **14302**: Hey, there! Peer deep within the Quests menu to find the "Objective List" under "Records of Eminence" and slap the objective there into place!
- **14303**: Attention! My name's Isakoth, and for today's lesson we're going to learn about...
- **14304**: Anything to say? [Impart to me your knowledge!/I don't have to listen to you./Nothing. Just walk away.]
- **14305**: Who gave you permission to leave!? A proper disciple listens to [his/her] master's advice at all times!
- **14306**: No one absolutely has to do anything, but I promise I have important and fun stuff to say. Everyone wants to have fun, right?
- **14307**: You're not going to get anywhere in life with that attitude. You need to learn how to cooperate, to go with the flow!
- **14308**: That's the spirit! You're an adventurer in my spitting image...at least when it comes to passion.
- **14309**: Today's lesson is all about "Records of Eminence," so break out your textbook and turn to page five!
- **14310**: What's that? Tsk, tsk, tsk. If you've forgotten your textbook, you'll have to share with the person next to you.
- **14311**: Let's get this show on the road. Records of Eminence is, in a word...
- **14312**: ...fighting spirit!
- **14313**: So what if that's two words!? That illustrates my point all the more. Adventurers need to give it eighty-thousand percent at all times if they want to uphold justice!
- **14314**: Class dismissed! Now go out there and do me proud.
- **14315**: Anything to say? [Yessir!/That's it?]
- **14316**: You can't fool me by just assenting to everything I say. That's not how education works. You'll never walk the path to enlightenment behaving like that!
- **14317**: That curiosity of yours will really take you places, [lad/lass]. It's positively indispensable for any child of the Goddess.
- **14318**: To tell the truth, though, you're already well on your way to prominence!
- **14319**: You even completed your first objective simply by talking to me. Furthermore, my experience tells me you'll be rewarded with some unusual type of compensation.
- **14320**: Now, take the next step on your journey and try setting an objective all by yourself!
- **14321**: No need to break out in a sweat--your eminent teacher will guide you. Just choose an objective under the "Objective List" in the "Records of Eminence" section of the Quests menu.
- **14322**: I hear you can even repeat certain ones. I'll let you figure out which ones they are on your own, though. I wouldn't want you to just regurgitate the answers I give you, now, would I?
- **14323**: Like I hinted at before, you gain "sparks of eminence" that quantify your achievements. Trade them to me and I'll give you back some tokens of gratitude.
- **14324**: Whoa, now. I teach more advanced subjects than you're able to understand at the moment. Try talking to Gulldago to learn the basics first.
- **14325**: By the way, this little bugger here, which we call $6, records all your eminent feats for you.
- **14326**: Hm? Oh. Yes, I too sometimes become unsettled by its constant and intense glaring.
- **14327**: Luckily, all you need to do is to clap your hands together like so and...
- **14328**: ...it vanishes into thin air. See, even this soulless machination possesses a fighting spirit. How else could it disappear instantaneously like that?
- **14329**: Just kidding. I'm only trying to cover up the fact that I don't know how it works. As my mentor always said, "When you don't know the answer to a question, distract the person with a joke."
- **14330**: Well, I've officially taught you all I know. Go out into the world with your trusty $3 and prove how esteemed you truly are!
- **14331**: ...I always get a little teary-eyed when I see my disciples go off to bigger and better things...<sniffle>
- **14338**: The $3 nods in agreement.
- **14341**: You wish to exchange your sparks?
- **14342**: Wait but a moment. Here, follow this command--er, compendium to the letter and you should be able to learn a thing or two.
- **14343**: Ah, this almost slipped my mind. Should you receive any distinct sorts of artifacts for completing an objective, I will be happy to take them off your hands.
- **14344**: Worry not, for I am not out to rob you of your glory. Quite the opposite, in fact. I will provide you with a worthwhile reward for collecting them.
- **14345**: Thanks to your donation, I now have $1 $0 in safekeeping.
- **14346**: Ohohoho... Turning in sparks, I see.
- **14347**: Hold your hoarder hares, [hero/heroine]. I love your enthusiasm, but you're not going to learn a thing without a primer. Ahhh, here it is. Have a look-see.
- **14348**: Oh, crud, that's right. You should trade me any special artifacts you get from completing objectives.
- **14349**: "Why?" What a good question! Because I'll give you a nifty-difty reward at some point, that's why.
- **14350**: Thanks for doing what I asked. Now I have a total of $1 $0 from you.
- **14351**: You want to trrrade in sparks, do you?
- **14352**: Let me wake up from my catnap first... Alright, now rrread this.
- **14353**: Wait, wait, wait! If you happen to get any purrrticularly interesting items from completing objectives, send them my way.
- **14354**: Don't worry, I'm as honest as a cat when the meat's out of reach. I thought of taking them all for myself...
- **14355**: ...but I don't want to make fur fly with the Adventurers' Mutual Aid Network, so I'll do my job like a good little kitty.
- **14356**: Grrreat! Now I have $1 $0 from you. Thanks!
- **14357**: Hm? Oh, spark exchange... Of course.
- **14358**: I'm not completely sure, but this might help set some things straight...I guess.
- **14359**: Let me add one thing. Please find it within your heart of hearts to give me anything of particular interest you get from completing objectives.
- **14360**: I'll make it worth your while...I swear! Just keep at it and I'll be sure to surprise you with a hot, steaming helping of gratitude one day.
- **14361**: Thanks for doing your part in the battle for love and peace! With this latest addition, I'm now keeping track of $1 $0 for you.
- **14362**: "Records of Eminence is a system brought to you by the caring folks at the Adventurers' Mutual Aid Network. Set your own objectives whenever you desire, and fulfill them to receive compensation.
- **14363**: "To set these tasks, select 'Objective List' from the Quests menu, then choose ones that suit your fancy.
- **14364**: "You will be rewarded with gil and sparks of eminence, which serve the same function as points, notes, credits, and other such methods of quantifying one's greatness. As an extra incentive, adventurers will earn three times the amount of sparks for completing an objective the first time.
- **14365**: "Completed objectives will vanish from your quest list unless they are repeatable. In the event that you wish to make room for different objectives, you will need to remove them manually.
- **14366**: "As a corollary regarding rewards, you'll be blessed with special remuneration once you complete several objectives. Bask in the joy of these pleasant surprises when you can.
- **14367**: "Lastly, any and all acts of vandalism directed against your $3 are strictly prohibited.
- **14368**: "Should you be found in violation of this dictum, the heavens will part and wrath incarnate will descend to mete out justice upon you."
